# Fivetran Documentation

> Fivetran is a fully managed data movement platform that automates data pipelines
> from hundreds of sources to your data warehouse or lake.
> Sitemap: https://fivetran.com/docs-sitemap.xml

## Getting Started
- [Getting Started](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started.md): Our documentation is your first stop for tech support, table schemas, and setup guides to get your analytics stack up and running in days instead of months.
- [Quickstart](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/quickstart.md): Learn how to create, configure, and navigate your new Fivetran account.
- [Free Trials](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/free-trials.md): Learn about Fivetran's free trials.
- [Account Trial](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/free-trials/account-trial.md): Learn about Fivetran's 14-day free trial for new accounts.
- [Use Your Own Destination](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/free-trials/account-trial/own-destination.md): Set up your free trial using your own destination.
- [New Connection Trial](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/free-trials/new-connector-free-trial.md): Learn about Fivetran's 14-day trial period for new connections.
- [Fivetran Dashboard](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard.md): Learn how to use your Fivetran dashboard.
- [Connections](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/connectors.md): Learn how to manage your connections in your Fivetran dashboard.
- [Status](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/connectors/status.md): Learn about the connector page and its features in the Fivetran dashboard.
- [Schemas](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/connectors/schema.md): Learn about how to view your connection schemas in the Fivetran dashboard.
- [Usage](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/connectors/usage.md): Learn how to view your connection MAR usage in the Fivetran dashboard.
- [Insights](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/connectors/insights.md): Learn about the Insights tab in your Fivetran dashboard and how it highlights the value Fivetran provides for a connection.
- [Transformations](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/transformations.md): Learn how to manage your Transformations for dbt Core in your Fivetran dashboard.
- [Destinations](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/destination.md): Learn how to manage your destinations in your Fivetran dashboard.
- [Alerts](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/alerts.md): Learn how to manage your Fivetran dashboard alerts.

- [Customer-Managed Keys](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/account-settings/cmk.md): Overview of the customer-managed key feature.
- [AWS CMK Setup Guide](https://fivetran.com/docs/getting-started/fivetran-dashboard/account-settings/cmk/aws-setup-guide.md): Read step-by-step instructions on how to integrate AWS customer-managed keys into Fivetran's pipeline.

## Core Concepts
- [Core Concepts](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts.md): Learn about the fundamentals of tables and schemas in relation to Fivetran connectors. Understand our approach to ETL, transformations, data cleaning, and mapping.
- [Product Principles](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/product-principles.md): Overview of Fivetran's product principles.
- [Deployment Models](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models.md): Learn about the architecture, key features, and benefits of SaaS and Hybrid deployment models and how each aligns with your specific business needs for data integration.
- [SaaS Deployment](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/saas-deployment.md): Discover Fivetran's SaaS Deployment model, a fully managed, cloud-based solution that simplifies seamless data integration from multiple sources to your destinations, featuring multi-source connectivity, diverse connector types, efficient data preparation, and robust security measures.
- [Hybrid Deployment](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment.md): Explore Fivetran Hybrid Deployment model for secure, automated data integration across cloud and on-premises environments. Maintain full control over your ELT pipelines with enhanced privacy and flexibility.
- [Setup Guide - Docker](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/setup-guide-docker.md): Learn how to set up Fivetran Hybrid Deployment with Docker. Follow step-by-step instructions for secure, local ELT pipeline configuration, and agent installation.
- [Setup Guide - Kubernetes](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/setup-guide-kubernetes.md): Deploy Fivetran Hybrid Deployment on Kubernetes using Helm. Follow our step-by-step guide to run ELT pipelines in your cluster with persistent storage and outbound connectivity.
- [Setup Guide - Podman](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/setup-guide-podman.md): Learn how to set up Fivetran Hybrid Deployment with Podman. Follow step-by-step instructions for secure, local ELT pipeline configuration, and agent installation.
- [Agent Configuration Parameters](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/agent-configuration-parameters.md): Learn about the parameters that you can configure to customize the Hybrid Deployment Agent for Docker, Kubernetes, and Podman.
- [Hybrid Deployment Troubleshooting](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting.md): Learn how to troubleshoot common issues with Hybrid Deployment.
- [Agent Failed to Start Job](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting/could-not-start-job-in-kubernetes.md): This article explains how to fix errors that prevent the agent from starting a job in a Kubernetes environment.
- [Agent Failed to Start Job or Container](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting/could-not-start-jobs.md): Learn how to resolve issues when the agent fails to start a job or container in Docker or Podman environment.
- [Agent Failed to Start Pipeline Job](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting/could-not-start-pipeline.md): Learn how to fix the issue when the Hybrid Deployment Agent cannot start a pipeline processing job.
- [Agent Running Sync Jobs](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting/hybrid-deployment-agents-for-sync-jobs.md): Find out if any Hybrid Deployment Agent can run your sync jobs.
- [Agents per Kubernetes Namespace](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting/agents-per-namespace.md): Learn about the Hybrid Deployment Agent namespace constraints in Kubernetes.
- [Avoiding Network Limits for Concurrent Jobs](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting/avoid-network-limits-to-run-jobs.md): Learn how to configure Hybrid Deployment to avoid Docker or Podman network limits when running multiple concurrent jobs on a single host.
- [AWS EC2 Spot Instances](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting/aws-ec2-spot-instance.md): Learn about AWS EC2 Spot instance support in Hybrid Deployment.
- [AWS S3 for Persistent Storage](https://fivetran.com/docs/core-concepts/deployment-models/hybrid-deployment/troubleshooting/aws-s3-for-kubernetes-storage.md): Learn about AWS S3 support for Kubernetes persistent storage in Hybrid Deployment.
